## Rule review

Every Quick Reference rule is represented below with its actual impact. Named target groups refer to exact arrays in evidence. E01/E09 are partial automatic enforcement and regression calibration; the semantic evidence records complementary obligations and limits. New/moved owners require a separate phase-2 delta.

Required sources start in sibling Suspense boundaries; changing scopes use non-suspending queries with explicit stale/error status. ## Existing decisions and ignores

Seven baseline architecture decisions remain supported (semantic-review.md). 143 existing inline ESLint exceptions retain their technical-review status and dates: **existing ignore not revalidated**. Normal metadata, tracking and unused-disable checks remain active and passed. Generated/vendor exclusions and rule-defined test/story/infrastructure scopes are not claimed as reviewed application compliance.

## Pending inspection

No baseline inspection remains pending. F-001/F-002/F-003 are resolved by verified P-001/P-002/P-003. Phase-2 additions/extractions and changed fixture/contract consumers have current evidence below; no inspection or required decision remains pending.

## Phase-2 owner delta

The original inventory above is preserved. Current source adds ten files (five runtime owners and five tests); the final complete file/hash manifest is [final-files.json](evidence/final-files.json). Changed contracts and caller obligations are reviewed in [introduced owner evidence](evidence/introduced-owner-review.md); fixtures in [fixture review delta](evidence/fixture-review-delta.md). No new query, atom, mutation, form, external link or translation key was introduced, so original non-applicability for those capabilities remains factually valid. Explicit typed prepared totals, pure helper inputs, semantic labels/nested children, collection preservation and nominal/edge test obligations were freshly checked for actual additions.
